Arcana arcanissima (Michael Maier, 1614)
Public domain. Sourced from Internet Archive: the 1614 Latin first edition (Hieroglyphica Aegyptio-Graeca) (https://archive.org/details/arcanaarcanissim00maie). Maier's mytho-alchemical masterwork: the most secret arcana decoded through Egyptian-Greek hieroglyphs and the fables of the golden age — the Rosicrucian physician's grand reading of mythology as chymical allegory, in Latin.
Hosted by the Tower of Babel library, antarctic-labs.com.
